847 entries, screen-manual to screenshot. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **screen-manual**: Web 画面の操作マニュアルを、Claude in Chrome で実画面を撮りながら作り、注釈付きキャプチャごと Notion ページに書き出す。操作手順書、設定マニュアル、利用ガイド、手順を Notion にまとめて、 画面キャプチャ付きで説明して、顧客向けの手順書、と言われたら使う。対象サービスがセキュアSAMBA でなくても使う。 `/screen-manual &lt;対象URL&gt; &lt;Notion URL&gt;` でも起動する。 リポジトリから全機能網羅の HTML マニュアルを生成する依頼は end-user-manual の担当で対象外。 動画での操作説明は record-browser の担当で対象外。 ([takeshiemoto/dotfiles](https://github.com/takeshiemoto/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dot_claude/skills/screen-manual))
- **screen-map**: Build and keep a product screen map that every builder follows as the source of truth: one page with every screen per platform, real copy, a status per screen (exists, built to test, proposal), arrows for the flow, simulations of real users, a style guide, and a project skill generated from it. Use when asked for a map of the screens, a UI flow, a source of truth for app, menu, site or onboarding, to turn a map into a skill, or to keep design and code aligned, also in Italian (mappa delle schermate, flusso, schermate dell'app, fonte di verità, trasforma la mappa in una skill), in any project. ([emanueledenaro/claude-skills](https://github.com/emanueledenaro/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/screen-map))
